# Infernal (Mob Type)
Categories: More info needed, Mob Types
[Infernal (Mob Type)]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in the Crimson Isle.
— Ike
Infernal mobs are a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]], mostly found in the [[Crimson Isle]]. The [[Smoldering]] enchantment, the [[Demonslayer Gauntlet]] and the [[Attributes|Infernal Ruler]] attribute all specifically increase damage against Infernal mobs. The [[Nether Artifact]] reduces the damage taken from Infernal mobs.

# Mana Regen
Aliases: Mana Regeneration
Categories: More info needed, Player Statistics, Mechanics
[Mana Regen]
Type: Stat
Uses: Increases the base rate at which one recovers mana in Rift.
Increasing: [[Armor]], [[Enigma Cloak]], [[Montezuma Pet]] and more
[Values]
Base Value: [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
Max Value: N/A
[Symbol]
Symbol: ⚡
Unicode Code Point: U+26A1
Icon (The icon displayed in the Equipment Menu interface.): Diamond ()
Mana Regen is a [[Stats|Rift Stat]]. It determines how fast the player's [[Mana]] regenerates.
## Mechanics
The player has a base Mana Regen of 0, which can be increased through various items. The higher the player's Mana Regen, the faster their [[Mana]] regenerates in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Increasing Bonus Mana Regen
### Armor
Some sets of [[Armor]] grant Mana Regen while equipped.
|Slot|Item|Rarity|Type|||
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Orange Chestplate]]|[[Orange Chestplate]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Chestplate ([[Chestplates]])|+10%|+10%|
|[[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]]|[[Lively Sepulture Chestplate]]|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Chestplate ([[Chestplates]])|+25%|+25%|

# Mayor Election
Aliases: Dante Election, Election, Mayor, Mayor Elections, Mayors, Mayors and Ministers, Minister, Minister Perk, Ministers, Special Mayor
Categories: More info needed, Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed, Mayor, Events, Mayor Election
Mayor Election
Election Period
:
Countdown:
Dates and times based on browser time.
✩ Current Mayor
[[Cole|Mayor Cole]] Perks: Prospection (Mining minions work 25% faster.) Mining XP Buff (Get +60☯ Mining Wisdom on public islands.) Mining Fiesta (Gain +25☯ Mining Wisdom, +25% Mining Fortune, and collect Refined Minerals and Glossy Gemstones from mining!)
[[Cole]]
[[Mayor Election/Events|➤ View Current Mayor/Election]]
  Not to be confused with [[Chocolate Rabbits|Mayor]] and [[Chocolate Rabbits|Minister]], City Faction Rabbits.
Mayors are [[NPC|NPCs]] that give global buffs and perks when elected by players. Mayor Elections were added in Hypixel SkyBlock's 0.9 Update, with the first election taking place in SkyBlock Year 88. Since then, they happened regularly, with few exceptions caused by events related to [[Dante]] and later [[Aura]].
## Mechanic
### Elections
An election is held every SkyBlock Year (124 real-life hours) with 5 candidates. Players vote by entering the [[Election Room]] via the teleport pad in the [[Community Center]], or calling [[Clerk Seraphine]] on an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]], while the voting period is open. A player can cast their votes to 1 candidate per account, as opposed to per [[Profile]], but they can change their chosen candidate anytime.
- The amount of votes players can cast can be increased via:
  - The player's [[Fame Ranks|Fame Rank]].
  - [[Voter's Badge]] and its upgrades.
  - Doubled with the [[Artifact of Control]].
  The maximum votes a player can get is [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.).
- Perks Distribution, followed by Candidate Selection, will happen before an election opens (see sections below).
- 5 candidates are revealed when the election starts on 27th of Late Summer at 00:00. The election will end on 27th of Late Spring 00:00 the following Year, at which point the candidate with the most votes will become the mayor.
### Mayors and Ministers
- Out of the 5 candidates, the one with highest amount of votes will have all of their shown perks active until the end of the next election.
- The leading candidate will become the Mayor and won't be able to participate in the following election. They will be stripped of their perks and one will be randomly selected the next time they appear.
- The runner-up will become a Minister, activating their minister perk alongside the Mayor's perks. They will also be unable to participate in the following election, unless [[Diaz]] has her Long Term Investment perk active.
  - Special Mayors cannot be Ministers. If runner-up is a Special Mayor, [[Nancy|Mayor Nancy]] will appear in a place of a Minister instead.
  - This also means that Long Term Investment perk doesn't work on Special Mayors.
- If the elected Mayor is a Special Mayor, there will be no Minister at all and [[Nancy|Mayor Nancy]] will appear in Minister's place in the [[Community Center]] as a placeholder.
  - In case of a tie, winning Mayor is determined by the candidate order.
### Perks Distribution
All candidates have multiple possible perks — most commonly 4 perks, with [[Paul]] and [[Aatrox]] not being reworked into this system yet.
Before an election, for all Regular Candidates (whether or not they are randomly chosen for the next election), perks are distributed in the following way:
- If a candidate was elected and is running as mayor, they will have no chance to participate or gain a perk on the election. On the election they next appear in, they will reset to 1 random perk with no possibility to gain a perk.
- If a candidate was not elected, all previous perks gained by that candidate are persistent (not wiped nor randomized in the future). Before the next election, whether or not they will participate the next election, they have a 50% chance to gain an additional perk. This chance is increased to 100% if they did not gain a perk in the last election they appeared in.
- If a candidate was elected Minister, they will have no chance to participate in the next election unless [[Diaz]] has her Long Term Investment perk active. However, all the perks they had up until that point are kept.
1 random perk of each candidate, except Special ones, is selected as Minister perk. This perk will be active even if the candidate ends up second. Minister perks are determined randomly and next time a candidate appears in an election, it may differ.
A Regular Candidate can participate in an election with any number of their perks. A Special and One-time Candidate always participate with all of their possible perks by default.
### Candidate Selection
The Regular Candidates are chosen randomly from the pool of all Regular candidates, except the one who is in the office as Mayor when the election starts. When no Special Candidate runs, 5 Regular Candidates are chosen.
Special candidates participate in an election not by random chance, but every [[Time Systems|8 SkyBlock years]] (when the SkyBlock Year is divisible by 8, and become a mayor the Year after).
- Special candidates always appear on the rightmost in the Election Interface.
- 4 other Regular Candidates are chosen.
- Special Mayors follow a set order [[Scorpius]] → [[Derpy]] → [[Jerry]] and then [[Scorpius]] again, this means that each Special candidate shows up in an election every 24 SkyBlock years.
- Derpy broke this pattern twice:
  - in the Year 128 [[Dante]] ran instead of him
  - in the Year 464, [[Aura]] was in office, which overwrote the standard election system.
- Previous and Upcoming Special mayors can be seen in the table below.
Special Candidates Timetable (Speculated):
|Candidate|Previous and Next Participation|
|-|-|
|[[Derpy]]|Year 512 ➜ 536|
|Jerry ([[Candidate Jerry]])|Year 520 ➜ 544|
|[[Scorpius]]|Year 504 ➜ 528|
## Candidates
These Mayor Candidates may join current and upcoming mayor elections.
### Regular Candidates
- [[Aatrox]] Maddox's Brother-in-Law
  1. SLASHED Pricing: Starting [[Slayer]] quests is half price.
  2. Slayer XP Buff: Earn 25% more [[Slayer]] XP.
  3. Pathfinder: Gain rare drops 20% more often.
- [[Cole]] the Miner
  1. Prospection: [[Mining]] minions work 25% faster.
  2. Mining XP Buff: Get +60 [[Mining Wisdom]] on public islands.
  3. [[Mining Fiesta]]: Grants +25 [[Mining Wisdom]], +25% [[Mining Fortune]] and ability to drop [[Refined Mineral]] and [[Glossy Gemstone]] from mining. [[Fiesta Flask]] and [[Packrat Shard]] can be bought from [[Brynmor]] while the perk is active.
  4. Molten Forge: Decrease the time it takes to [[The Forge|forge]] items by 25%.
- [[Diana]] the Mythological Pet Caretaker
  1. Huntress' Intuition: Gain +10 [[Tracking]]
  2. [[Mythological Ritual]]: Mayor Diana will sell the [[Griffin Pet]], which lets you find [[Mythological|Mythological Creatures]] and tons of unique items.
  3. Pet XP Buff: Gain +35% more pet XP.
  4. Sharing is Caring: You can have up to 3 EXP Shared Pets active at once. Your EXP Share rate is increased by 10%.
- [[Diaz]] the Economist
  1. Long Term Investment: The elected [[Mayor Election|Minister]] will appear in the next [[Mayor Election|Election]] with all of their available perks.
  2. Volume Trading: Double the item quantity from [[Shen's Auction]], Shen's Special, and Rift Shen's on the year Diaz is elected. Two additional Shen's Special auctions will be available for the duration of Diaz being elected as Mayor.
  3. [[Richard|Stock Exchange]]: Participate in the Stonks Auction for a chance to win [[Stock of Stonks]]! Trade them for extravagant items at the [[Trade Center]].
  4. Shopping Spree: Increase daily [[NPC]] buy limits by 10x.
- [[Finnegan]]
  1. Pest Eradicator: The duration of [[Pesthunter Phillip]]'s [[Farming Fortune]] bonus is now 60m. Decreases the spawn cooldown of [[Pests]] by 20%.
  2. GOATed: [[Jacob's Farming Contest]] brackets include up to 10% more players each.
  3. Blooming Business: [[The Garden|Garden Visitors]] will give out [[Fine Flour]] and appear more often. Additional visitors may visit your [[The Garden|Garden]]. Higher rarity Visitors are more likely to show up & will provide 10% more [[Copper]].
  4. Grand Feast: Finnegan upgrades the [[Harvest Feast]] into the [[Harvest Feast|Grand Feast]], making it last a full SkyBlock year. Also opens the [[Communal Stew|Grand Bakery]], where [[Kernels]] can be spent on more exclusive items.
- [[Foxy]] the Event Girl
  1. Sweet Benevolence: Earn +30% more [[Spooky Festival|Candy]], [[Gifts]] and [[Chocolate]] from duplicate [[Chocolate Rabbits|Rabbits]] during their respective events.
  2. Extra Event: Schedules an extra event during the year, out of: [[Spooky Festival]], [[Mining Fiesta]], and [[Fishing Festival]].
  3. A Time for Giving: [[Party Chest|Party Chests]] and [[Party Gift|Party Gifts]] can be obtained while this event is active.
  4. [[Carnival|Chivalrous Carnival]]: Schedules a [[Carnival]] in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] that is active throughout the entire year.
- [[Marina]] the Fisherwoman
  1. Fishing XP Buff: Gain 50 [[Fishing Wisdom]] on public islands.
  2. Luck of the Sea 2.0: Gain 15 [[Sea Creature Chance]].
  3. [[Fishing Festival]]: Start a special fishing event during the first 3 days of each month! Fish and fight dangerous [[Sharks (disambiguation)|Sharks]] and earn unique Shark loot.
  4. Double Trouble: For every 1 [[Sea Creature Chance]], gain +0.1 [[Double Hook Chance]].
- [[Paul]] the Dungeon Explorer
  1. Marauder: [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon reward chests]] are 20% cheaper.
  2. EZPZ: Gain 10 extra bonus score in [[Dungeons]].
  3. Benediction: [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessings]] are 25% stronger.
### Special Candidates
- [[Derpy]]
  1. QUAD TAXES!!!: Pay 4x the normal amount of taxes!
  2. TURBO MINIONS!!!: Minions have double the output!
  3. DOUBLE MOBS HP!!!: ALL monsters have double health!
  4. MOAR SKILLZ!!!: Gain +50% more skill experience!
- [[Candidate Jerry|Jerry]]
  1. [[Candidate Jerry/Perkpocalypse]]: Activates all perks of another mayor every 18 SkyBlock days (6 hours).
  2. Statspocalypse: Increases all [[Stats|Combat]] and [[Stats|Miscellaneous]] stats by 10%.
  3. Jerrypocalypse: Reveal hidden Jerries from logging, farming, mining and killing mobs.
- [[Scorpius]]
  1. Bribe: If Scorpius wins and you voted for him, Mayor Scorpius will offer you 50,000 [[Coins]] - 1,000,000 [[Coins]] as a token of gratitude.
  2. Darker Auctions: Scorpius will intrude in [[Dark Auction|Dark Auctions]], increasing the number of rounds to 7 and offering special items.
These Mayor Candidates participated elections in the past, but will not participate in future elections.
### Retired Regular Candidates

  - Listed Perks:

    2. Astral Negotiator: Enchanting and anvils costs -15% less experience.
    3. Magic XP Boost: Gain +15 [[Alchemy Wisdom]] and [[Enchanting Wisdom]]

    2. Astral Negotiator: Enchanting and anvils costs -15% less experience. Secret Bonus: Gain +1 [[Magic Find]] for every year that Barry has not been elected (max 20)

  - Listed Perks:

    2. Increased Loot*:
    3. Increased Experience*:
  - Actual Perks: (hidden until Aura is elected)
    1. Fundraising: Taxes and Costs vastly increased across the world.
    2. Minion Union: Minions work +50% slower.
    3. Universal Income: Every hour, 100% of the [[Coins]] gained from Fundraising are redistributed equally.
    4. Work Better: Everyone is now luckier!
    5. Work Harder: Monsters deal 10% more damage and have 10% more health.
    6. Work Smarter: Gain +50% Skill EXP, Dungeon EXP and Slayer EXP
- [[Dante]]
  - Listed Perks:

    2. Security: Strengthen security
    3. Freedom: Freedom from fear

      - Double AH and Bazaar taxes
      - 10% coin tax on shops
      - 5 coins per minute for chatting
      - 2 coin toll on travelling between islands
    2. Increase Security:
      - Hired goons to protect official buildings
      - Cancelled hazardous events

- [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]]
  1. Blood God's Blessing: +50% Skill XP
  2. Shrine Of The Blood God: Summons the Blood God Shrine and its [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]] (This perk continued after Technoblade's term as the shiny relics were not all found)
  3. Unlimited Speed: Gives +50 [[Speed]] and removes the speed cap (This perk was bugged and did not seem to work, thus being removed halfway through Technoblade’s mayor term)
  4. Potato Crown's Radiance: Farming Minions have double output (This perk was bugged and seemed to work on all minions, rather than only farming minions)
  5. Anarchy: There are no taxes in the [[Auction House]] and [[Bazaar]] (This perk was shortly removed after Technoblade was elected, likely that the admins thought no taxes would cause excessive inflation)

## Interface
This is a simplified interactable example of the election interface.
## The Uprising
A Dante Election
Interface
While [[Dante]] was in the office after being elected, all following elections events scheduled in the [[Calendar|Calendar and Events]] menu became temporarily canceled.
Regular mayor elections were replaced with Dante Elections, in which all of the 5 candidates were replaced by Dante's clones — voting for any of them results in the vote being applied to all candidates. That means that the vote couldn't be changed and the election result was always 100% for Dante.
There used to be a bug where [[Clerk Seraphine]] was the clerk present in the [[Election Room]] instead of [[Clerk Goon]], however, it was fixed.

## On the Alpha Hypixel Network
Generally, when [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] opens, Mayor Elections are fully-functional and are held separately from the Main Server.
### Mechanic
Mayor Elections on AHN run with the same principles, but independantly from those on the main server:
- The results are counted separately, the candidates and their perks are picked separately.
- The time that an Election starts and ends is in sync with the main server.
- When the Alpha Hypixel Network is open, players can vote on its Mayor Election once per account (not per profile), regardless of whether they voted on the main server.
#### On an Alpha Network Reset
- At the time of a [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Network Reset]], [[Nancy|Mayor Nancy]] is the placeholder mayor for Alpha Hypixel Network until the next election.
- If an election is supposed to be happening after a reset, the election feature will be disabled until the next election[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]. See #Election Feature Disabled.
#### On a Session Opening
- When the Alpha Network closes and reopens without a Network Reset, all aspects of the Mayor Election will not be affected.
- If an election is skipped during the downtime of the Alpha Network, it is unknown whether a random Regular candidate will be selected as mayor, or the previously elected mayor, or [[Nancy|Mayor Nancy]] being used as placeholder[More Info Needed].
### Previous Elections
Due to the difficulty presented in recording Mayor Elections on the Alpha Hypixel Network which occasionally shuts down and reopens, it will be documented separately on [[Mayor Election/Alpha|Mayor Election/Alpha Hypixel Network]], and not integrated to the wiki's main table.
## Features Disabled
### Mayor Disabled
There are some occasions during which a Mayor is disabled. This may happen when:
- The previous mayor is not replaced after an election on a [[Hub Island|Hub]] server
- The election feature is disabled on the Alpha Hypixel Network
The following is the observed behavior:
- When interacted, the "mayor" NPC will only give [NPC] Mayor <Name>: Hello!!
### Election Feature Disabled
On some occasions, the mayor election feature can be disabled on the Alpha Network. This is the observed behavior:
- The Election Room is still present when votings happens in the main server. However, it will almost instantly teleport the player out when they attempt to get in. If there is enough ping, a player can interact with the NPCs and Ballot Box inside before it teleports you out.
- Election is "closed". However, the timer outside the Election Room will still run in sync with the Main Server.
- Ballot Box and Seraphine dialogue follows the version when election is closed.
  - Attempting to click on the box in the middle of the [[Election Room]] when the election is closed will show the message The election booth is closed!
## Notes
- An election that starts during Year (Year X) will end during the next Year (Year X+1), as shown in the election interface (image proof).
  - The election is always called "Election Year X".
  - In each candidate, the sequence of a candidate's perks can change when a Year passes. However, the perks will not change.
  - In each candidate, the number of years shown in "last elected" will increment when a Year passes.
## Trivia
- [[Derpy]] was the first candidate to have 4 perks.
- [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] is the only mayor to have 5 perks and technically 11 perks.
- [[Candidate Jerry|Jerry]] becoming a mayor was leaked by Minikloon on his Twitter. Additionally, Dctr leaked the first perk, "Perkpocalypse" on his Twitter a day later.
- [[Candidate Jerry|Jerry]]'s Perkpocalypse does not include Special mayors, candidates no longer participating or [[Diaz]].
- Before 0.20.3 [[Diaz]] had only 2 perks, one of which got removed and was replaced by 3 new ones.
- [[Diaz]]'s perk, "Shopping Spree" also applies to [[Trades]].
- [[Dante]] is the only candidate to swap perks after the election.
  - Although, when elected, [[Barry]] had a secret bonus to all of his perks.
- Barry was first elected in Year 188, 100 SkyBlock Years after the first election, making him the mayor who went the longest time without being elected.
  - [[Diaz]] was another Regular candidate that had never been elected before. She was elected for the first time on June 30 2021, during the Year 146 Election, making her and Barry the two mayors who went the longest without being elected.
- [[Barry]] and [[Candidate Jerry|Jerry]] are the only candidates that buff damage.
  - [[Candidate Jerry|Jerry]] has +10% Combat and Miscellaneous stat boost.
  - [[Barry]] had +15% increased spell damage.
- If no Mayor is elected for any reason, [[Nancy|Mayor Nancy]] will appear as a placeholder.
- [[Dante]], [[Barry]] and [[Candidate Aura|Aura]] are the only candidates that had unlisted perks that were revealed when elected.
- No Special mayor has ever lost an election.
- In Year 241 an extra mayor was added to the mayor results with 0 votes. This was the new Mayor [[Finnegan]] who was added in the 0.16.1 Update (image)
- There was a theory that when [[Diaz]] is elected as Mayor with the perk Long Term-investment, and a Special Mayor candidate is in 2nd place, the Special Mayor would have no perks for the Year, and would show up with full perks the next Year.
  - This was seemingly confirmed by 55[[Ranks|[ዞ]]] TimeDeo after Diaz received 25% of the votes against Derpy, according to 7[[Ranks|[YOUTUBE]]] Derailious.
    - This was later disproven by another admin.

# Mutations

[Mutations]
Mutations are a type of crop created in the [[Greenhouse]]. They are created by arranging crops in specific patterns around an empty soil plot and grant special positive and/or negative effects.
Mutations can be analyzed in the [[Crop Analyzer]], accessed either via the [[Underground Lab]] or by calling [[Jake]] on an [[Abiphones|Abiphone]]. Analyzing a mutation for the first time grants bonus [[Copper]] and contributes to the [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestone]].
Mutations can be stored in the [[Mutations Sack]]. The recipes for it are unlocked at [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestone I (1)]], [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestone III (3)]], and [[Crop Analyzer|DNA Analysis Milestone V (5)]].

## Modifiers
This section needs more information. Please help expand it.
See more stubs.
Placed Mutations affect their surrounding Mutations and Crops. These effects will lock once the Mutation or Crop reach their final stage.
Planting unique Crops in [[Greenhouse]] will give +2.5% Yield and +2.5% Growth Speed per, up to 10 times ( [[Moonflower]] and [[Sunflower]] count as the same crop, same for [[Mushroom|Red Mushroom]] and [[Mushroom|Brown Mushroom]]). This bonus can also be obtained with the [[Flora Shard]].
## Tips
- Players can enable the Confirm breaking ungrown Mutations in their [[Settings]] menu, which can help prevent accidentaly breaking some Mutations.
  - This setting only applies to Mutations that the player planted themselves, not Mutations that spread naturally. This may or may not be a bug.[More Info Needed]

[Mythological]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Mythological is a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]] for mobs that are found exclusively during the [[Mythological Ritual]]. They spawn when excavating [[Griffin Burrows]] with an [[Ancestral Spade|Ancestral]], [[Archaic Spade|Archaic]], and [[Deific Spade]].
## Behavior
Mythological mobs have a 75% chance to spawn from any [[Griffin Burrows|Griffin Burrow]] in a chain except for the first one, and require a matching or higher tier of [[Griffin Pet]] as the one they were spawned with to damage. They despawn after five minutes if they are not killed, or if the player who spawned them dies.
## List
The types of Mythological mobs that can be dig up from a [[Griffin Burrows|Griffin Burrow]] varies depending on the rarity of the player's [[Griffin Pet]].
Listed spawn chances are base chances. [[Tracking]] increases the weight of [[Elusive]] mobs. For example, having 15 [[Tracking]] would multiply their weights by 1.15x.
In addition to the stats listed below, all Mythological mobs have 50% resistance to [[Damage Calculation|Magic Damage]].
[[Gaia Construct|Gaia Constructs]] as well as all mobs that first appear in [[Rarity|EPIC]] tier or above are immune to damage from bows,[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] with the exception of [[King Minos]]' hit shield.

## Increasing stats against Mythological
There are many ways to increase stats against Mythological enemies.

|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sword of Revelations]]|[[Sword of Revelations]]|Sword ([[Swords]])|Deals x1.5 [[Damage]] to Mythological mobs.|Multiplicative (Multiplicative buffs are simple multipliers applied at the end, after flat and additive buffs.)|
|[[Daedalus Blade]]|[[Daedalus Blade]]|Sword ([[Swords]])|Grants +1% [[Damage]] on Mythological mobs per [[Bestiary]] tier you have across all of them. (Max +230%) Grants +1.5% [[Damage]] on Mythological mobs per [[Bestiary]] tier you have across all of them. (Max +345%)|(same as above)|
|[[Minos Hunter Shard]]|[[Minos Hunter Shard]] Mythological Ruler ([[Attributes]])|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Grants +3–39% more [[Damage]] against Mythological mobs.|Additive (Additive buffs are added together with each other, and then the sum is multiplied with the flat amount.)|

# Pest (Mob Type)
Categories: More info needed, Mob Types
[Pest (Mob Type)]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in The Garden.
— Ike
Pest mobs are a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]] which can only be damaged with [[Vacuums]].

# Runes
Aliases: Antlers Rune III, Bark Tunes Rune III, Barkshatter Rune III, Bite Rune, Blazing Sun Rune III, Blood Rune, Blooming Rune III, Clouds Rune, Couture Rune, Crowned Rune, Crowned Rune III, Darkness Within Rune, Darkness Within Rune I, Enchant Rune, End Rune, Endersnake Rune, Explosifall Rune III, Fading Blue Rune, Fading Green Rune, Fading Rainbow Rune, Fading Rainbow Rune III, Fading White Rune, Fiery Burst Rune, Fire Spiral Rune, Flowery Carpet Rune III, Flowing Fall Rune III, Gem Rune, Golden Carpet Rune III, Golden Rune, Grand Freezing Rune III, Grand Gardening Rune III, Grand Leaf-ing Rune III, Grand Searing Rune, Grand Searing Rune III, Harmony Rune, Harmony Rune III, Hearts Rune, Heartsplosion Rune III, Hot Rune, Ice Rune, Ice Skates Rune III, Jerry Rune, Lava Rune, Lavatears Rune, Lightning Rune, Magical Rune, Meow Music Rune III, Music Rune, Peafowl Rune, Peafowl Rune III, Pestilence Rune, Primal Fear Rune III, Rainbow Carpet Rune, Rainbow Carpet Rune III, Rainbow Rune, Rainy Day Rune, Rainy Day Rune III, Redstone Rune, Rune, Runic Mobs, Slimy Rune, Smitten Rune, Smitten Rune III, Smokey Rune, Snake Rune, Snow Rune, Soul Slice Rune III, Soultwist Rune, Sparkling Rune, Spellbound Rune III, Spirit Rune, Sunny Spiral Rune, Sunny Spiral Rune III, Super Pumpkin, Super Pumpkin Rune, Super Pumpkin Rune III, Tidal Rune, Turkey Rune, Turkey Rune III, Wake Rune, White Spiral Rune, Zap Rune
Categories: More info needed
[Runes]
Runes are cosmetics that can be applied to items to give them particle effects upon use.
## Runic Mobs
Runes are obtained from Runic Mobs. Runic Mobs are normal SkyBlock [[Mob|Mobs]], but with 4x higher [[Health]], and deal double [[Damage]]. They have a purple name tag, and a much lower chance to spawn.
- Mobs that can become Runic have a 0.5% chance of being so when they spawn in.
- They are guaranteed to drop a Rune.
- They have a 0.1% (1 in 1,000) chance to drop a [[Runebook]].
- They have a 1 in 100,000-500,000 chance to drop a [[Periwinkle Dye]], depending on their level.
All mobs can be Runic except [[Ender Dragon]], [[Crimson Isle|Crimson Isle Mini-bosses]], [[Slayer]] Boss/Miniboss, [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Boss]] or [[Catacombs Mobs|Dungeon Boss Minion]].
Certain runes are also obtained from [[Slayer]] or as special rewards.
## Runic Pedestal
Runes can be fused on the [[Runic Pedestal]] found behind the [[Blacksmith]] and owned by [[Dusk]].
Fusing two runes will have a chance of upgrading the rune to the next level, up to the maximum level of 3. Upon success or failure, the second rune is consumed. If this process does not succeed, the remaining rune will not gain a level. The chance of success can be seen in the Runic Pedestal while hovering over the apply trigger. Regardless of the outcome, the player will gain Runecrafting experience. More experience is awarded upon success.
Runes can be fused to Swords, [[Bows]], [[Chestplates]], [[Boots]], [[Helmets]] and [[Axes]], depending on the type of rune. The player's Runecrafting level must be greater than or equal to that of the rune, or they won't be able to fuse it. This only applies when fusing runes to items; the player can still fuse runes whose level is above their Runecrafting level. Fusing runes onto items will also grant Runecrafting experience.

## Rates
Different rune rarities grant differing amounts of experience and have different success rates when fusing.
|Rarity|Experience|Merge Chance|Expected Runes to Acquire T2|Expected Runes to Acquire T3|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10|25%|5|25|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|15|35%|3.86|14.88|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|25|40%|3.5|12.25|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|30|45%|3.22|10.38|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|35|50%|3|9|
F(n), the probability of successfully fusing on the nth attempt is represented as a function of P(n), the probability of merging on a given attempt. This distribution is binomial.
F(n)=P(n)*(1−P(n))n−1
C(n) the cost in runes on the nth attempt is
C(n)=1+n
Therefore, we can represent E(n), the expected number of runes during fusing as E(n) = ∑ F(n) * C(n)
E(n)=∑P(n)*(1−P(n))n−1*(1+n) To calculate the expected runes to acquire
T3 , we can represent the cost C(n) in terms of the cost of T2, or
C3(n)=E(n)+E(n)*n
Therefore, the cost of T3 rune is
E3(n)=∑P(n)*(1−P(n))n−1*(1+n)+n*∑P(n)*(1−P(n))n−1*(1+n) =E(n)2 Alternatively, where
L is the level of the rune desired:
EL(n)=(P(n)−1+1)L−1

# Rusty
Aliases: Janitor, Rusty the Janitor
Categories: More info needed, Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
[Rusty]
Type: [[NPC]]
Quests: Lost and Found ([[Quests]])
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Sells various one-time items that the player may have lost.
Location: [[Gold Mine]]
Coordinates: -19 78 -325.5
Rusty is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Gold Mine]]. He sells back one-time rewards from quests for a certain amount of [[Coins]].
For some upgradable items, Rusty sells all levels of the item. While for some upgradable items, he only sells the highest unlocked form of the item, as indicated in the list below.

## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction; SkyBlock Level 6+|[NPC] Rusty: Hi, I'm the janitor of this mine. [NPC] Rusty: You would not believe how many people leave ingots and stones behind them! [NPC] Rusty: It drives me insane, but at least you unlocked Auto-pickup. [NPC] Rusty: It makes my job a lot easier, but despite that, I still find so many items on the ground. [NPC] Rusty: Maybe some of those items are yours? In which case I'll let you buy them back.|
|First Interaction; Below SkyBlock Level 6|[NPC] Rusty: Hi, I'm the janitor of this mine. [NPC] Rusty: You would not believe how many people leave ingots and stones behind them! [NPC] Rusty: It drives me insane, but at least you'll unlock Auto-pickup at SkyBlock Level 6. [NPC] Rusty: It makes my job a lot easier, but despite that, I still find so many items on the ground. [NPC] Rusty: Maybe some of these items are yours? In which case I'll let you buy them back.|
|After Finding [[Lazy Miner]]'s Pickaxe|[NPC] Rusty: You found the Lazy Miner's pickaxe! [NPC] Rusty: I'll have to put it in my stores. [NPC] Rusty: Click me again!|
|Abiphone Contact ([[Abiphones]]) None|[NPC] Rusty: ✆ Did I find an Abiphone? [NPC] Rusty: ✆ Yes, sometimes I do find one lying around. [NPC] Rusty: ✆ What? [NPC] Rusty: ✆ You... [NPC] Rusty: ✆ You want my contact? [NPC] Rusty: ✆ Me? [NPC] Rusty: ✆ The janitor? [NPC] Rusty: ✆ I... [NPC] Rusty: ✆ I don't... don't know what to say... [NPC] Rusty: ✆ Yes of course you can have it!|
|[[The Garden]] None|[NPC] Rusty: Ever wondered how I find all the things that other players lost? I outsource it. Right now, someone is looking for their [crop]. Can you find that for me?|
|[[The Garden]] Completing|[NPC] Rusty: There we go, a job well done. I'll make sure to leave a good review for you!|
|[[The Garden]] Denying|[NPC] Rusty: Turns out, if you want something done, you have to do it yourself.|
|First Interaction; Before April 25, 2022|[NPC] Rusty: Hi, I'm the janitor of this mine. [NPC] Rusty: You would not believe how many people leave ingots and stones behind them! [NPC] Rusty: It drives me insane, everyone should be using Telekinesis on their tools! [NPC] Rusty: If you want I can apply Telekinesis on any of your items for 100 coins.|
## Trivia
- Rusty used to have no shop and only applied Telekinesis on tools for 100 [[Coins]]. This was changed alongside the introduction of [[Auto-pickup]].
- The [[White Belt]] (and its upgrades), [[Kuudra Follower Armor]], [[Campfire Badge|Campfire Initiate Badge]] (and its upgrades), [[Soul Campfire Badge|Soul Campfire Initiate Badge]] (and its upgrades), [[Book of Progression]], [[Ring of Love|Shiny Yellow Rock]] (and its upgrades), [[Beastslayer's Codex]], [[Kuudra Follower Artifact]] (and its upgrade, [[Kuudra Follower Relic]]), [[Kuudra Teeth Plaque]] and [[Safari Belt]] are not affected by the discount from [[Shady Ring]], [[Crooked Artifact]], or [[Seal of the Family]]. It is unknown if this is intentional or a bug.
- Buying [[Safari Belt]] will give the player the item with the exact same upgrades as their last modified one. [More Info Needed]

# Skill Experience Bonus
Aliases: Weekend Bonus
Categories: More info needed, Outdated pages, Mechanics, Historical content
This article needs to be reworked as its content is outdated. You can help the wiki by editing this page and reworking it.
This article was last edited by LunaBot (contribs|logs|block) on 8:36, June 4 2026 (4 months ago)
The Skill Experience Bonus is a global skill experience and [[Magic Find]] bonus for all the SkyBlock [[Player|Players]]. It was first introduced on the 27th of March 2020.
When the bonus is active, upon entering SkyBlock, the player was notified every time in chat that they were going to earn a certain percentage more Skill Experience. For example:
- "BONUS! Earn 20% more skill experience!"
## Occasions
### Description
On 27 March 2020, an admin Donpireso made a post Triple Coins/XP!. In this post, it was announced that:
For SkyBlock players, you will be earning 20% more Skill Experience, and everyone will have 10 more Magic Find!
The boosts are:
- 20% more Skill Experience
- +10 [[Magic Find]]
### Duration
The boost started around 27 March 2020. No information was given for how long the bonus was going to last. According to the post, the boost was extended for an unknown amount of time.
- The skill experience bonus ended on 21 October 2020. The total duration was 7 months.
- [[Magic Find]] boost ended on 20 November 2020. The total duration was 8 months.
### Description
On 23 December 2021, an admin ChiLynn made a post Triple Coins! Triple XP!. In this post, it was announced that:
A special present for SkyBlock players: 20% more Skill Exp and 10 more Magic Find for everyone!
The boosts are:
- 20% more Skill Experience
- +10 [[Magic Find]]
### Duration
The boost started around 23 December 2021. The boost ended on 19 January 2022. The total duration was 4 weeks.
### Description
Around 14 April 2023, a bonus was activated for the Hypixel 10th Anniversary Event.
The boosts are:
- 10% more Skill Experience
### Duration
The boost started around 14 April 2023. It is unknown when it ended.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
### Description
Around 18 December 2023, a bonus was activated for the Christmas holidays.
The boosts are:
- 10% more Skill Experience
### Duration
The boost started around 18 December 2023, and is still ongoing as of 28 December 2023.
## Trivia
- The +10 [[Magic Find]] boost does not show up in the in-game message.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Mar 27, 2020|Skill Experience Bonus was first announced.|
Linked from: [[Skill XP Calculation]]

# Sprays
Aliases: Spray
Categories: More info needed, Pages with broken file links, Confirmations needed, Cosmetics
[Sprays]
The [[Spray Can]] is used to print Sprays.
Sprays are cosmetic pictures that can be printed on the side of a block using a [[Spray Can]], and can be obtained mainly through [[Reward Bundles]].

# Taylor
Aliases: Seasonal Bundle, Seasonal Bundles, Taylor's Collection
Categories: More info needed, AHN, Shops, Abiphone NPCs, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs
This page contains content that has recently been updated on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], but that also exists on the main Hypixel network.
[Taylor]
Type: [[NPC]]
Shop: ✔ Yes
Abiphone ([[Abiphones]]): ✔ Yes
Description: Sells various cosmetics for [[SkyBlock Gems]].
Location: [[Taylor's Shop]]
Coordinates: 37.5 74 -39.5
Taylor is an [[NPC]] located inside the [[Taylor's Shop]].
## Usage
### Taylor's Cosmetics
Taylor sells both seasonally rotating and permanent items for [[SkyBlock Gems]]. Seasonal Bundles offer thematic [[Skin (disambiguation)|Skins]], [[Runes]], and [[Dyes]] during network wide seasonal events like Lunar New Years, Valentines, Summer, Fall, and Winter. Cosmetics from collections up to and including Fall 2024 will not return, while cosmetics from Winter 2024 and after may return in future bundles.
Taylor's Collection is similar to Seasonal Bundles. Cosmetics offered appear in a non-rotating stock of all year round.
### Garden
Taylor can visit the player's Garden once the player reaches Garden 14. She requests different resources for one of 2 skins:
- [[Barn Skins|Fast Food Barn Skin]]: 256 (4 stacks) [[Deepfries]], 256 [[Salted Sunflower Seeds]], and 3,200 (50 stacks) [[Enchanted Baked Potato|Enchanted Baked Potatoes]] (worth )
- [[Greenhouse Skins|Jelly Garden Greenhouse Skin]]: 256 [[Floral Gelatin]], 256 [[Melon Juice]], and 2,560 (40 stacks) [[Compacted Wild Rose|Compacted Wild Roses]] (worth )

# Treasure
Aliases: Fishing Treasure, Good Catch, Outstanding, Outstanding Catch, Treasure Fishing, Tresure
Categories: More info needed, Fishing
[Treasure]
Treasure is a type of [[Fishing]] loot. The chances of naturally fishing up Treasure is determined by the player's [[Treasure Chance]].
## Overview
Treasure refers to rare items that can be obtained from Fishing. Upon catching Treasure, a message will display in chat, showing the type of Treasure that was rolled.
The default chances for each catch type are as follows:
Type:
GOOD CATCH: 89% (1 in 1.12 treasure catches)
GREAT CATCH: 10% (1 in 10 treasure catches)
OUTSTANDING CATCH: 1% (1 in 100 treasure catches)
Some items can influence these chances, stacking additively:
|Slot|Name|Effect|
|-|-|-|
|[[Hermit Crab Pet]]|[[Hermit Crab Pet]]|10% more GREAT and OUTSTANDING catches.|
|[[Blessing (Enchantment)]]|Blessing ([[Blessing (Enchantment)]])|12% more GREAT and OUTSTANDING catches.|
|[[Blessed Bait]]|[[Blessed Bait]]|50% more GREAT and OUTSTANDING catches.|
|[[Mantis Shrimp Shard]]|Great Treasure ([[Mantis Shrimp Shard]])|10% more GREAT catches.|
|[[Blue Crab Shard]]|Outstanding Fisher ([[Blue Crab Shard]])|10% more OUTSTANDING catches.|
## Loot
- [[Treasure/Loot/Water|Water]]
- [[Treasure/Loot/Lotus Atoll|Lotus Atoll]]
- [[Treasure/Loot/Winter|Winter]]
- [[Treasure/Loot/Moonglade Marsh|Moonglade Marsh]]
- [[Treasure/Loot/Torrhus Canyon|Torrhus Canyon]]
- [[Treasure/Loot/Junk|Junk]]
- [[Treasure/Loot/Fairy Pond|Fairy Pond]]
- [[Treasure/Loot/Lava|Lava]]
- [[Treasure/Loot/Crimson Isle|Crimson Isle]]
Some items are rolled before some fishing loot under certain conditions, for example: [[Festive Sinker]] overrides all pools, including [[Sea Creatures]], with 5% (1 in 20 catches) chance, but [[Bouncy Beach Ball]] only overrides GOOD CATCH pool with 25% (1 in 4 catches) chance.
[[Treasure Dye]] has a chance to drop from any Treasure pool.
|Condition|Overriden pools|Item rolled|Chance to override|
|-|-|-|-|
|Any location|GOOD CATCH|[[Treasure Dye]]|0.0001% (1 in 1,000,000 catches)|
|Any location|GREAT CATCH|[[Treasure Dye]]|0.001% (1 in 100,000 catches)|
|Any location|OUTSTANDING CATCH|[[Treasure Dye]]|0.01% (1 in 10,000 catches)|
|Using [[Ice Bait]], wearing [[Elegant Tuxedo]] and; [[Bonzo's Mask]] with Respiration I ([[Respiration (Enchantment)]]) near [[Weird Sailor]]|All|[[Wet Napkin]] (once)||
|[[Festive Sinker]] while [[Jerry's Workshop]] is opened|All|[[Gifts]]|5% (1 in 20 catches)|
|[[Upper Mines]]|All|[[Treasurite]]|2% (1 in 50 catches)|
|[[Stereo Pants]]|All|Music Discs ([[Vanilla Objects/Music Discs]])|3% (1 in 33.3 catches)|
|[[Year of the Seal]]|GOOD CATCH|[[Bouncy Beach Ball]]|25% (1 in 4 catches)|
|[[Hotspot Haven]] Fishing Hotspot ([[Fishing Hotspots]])|GOOD CATCH|[[Giant Water Bug Shard]]|100%|
|[[Hotspot Haven]] Fishing Hotspot ([[Fishing Hotspots]])|GREAT CATCH|4–8 [[Giant Water Bug Shard]]|100%|
|[[Year of the Seal]]|GREAT CATCH|[[Giant Bouncy Beach Ball]]|15% (1 in 6.67 catches)|
|[[Abandoned Quarry]]|GREAT CATCH|[[Speckled Teacup]]|10% (1 in 10 catches)|
|[[Fairy Grotto]]|Treasure|Fairy Soul ([[Fairy Souls]]) (twice only)|0.5% (1 in 200 catches)|
|[[Fishing Crystal]]|[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)|[[Prismarine Crystals]] [[Prismarine Shard]]|[More Info Needed]|
|2nd, 9th and 24th treasure catch|Treasure|Fairy Soul ([[Fairy Souls]])||
|[[Wooden Bait]]|GOOD CATCH|See [[Wooden Bait]] page for more info|See [[Wooden Bait]] page for more info|
In some locations and with some attributes additional weighted items are added to some pools:
|Condition|Added to Pool|Item added|Added Weight|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Crystal Hollows]]|GOOD CATCH|[[Silentdepth Shard]]|0.5|
|[[Crimson Isle]]|GOOD CATCH|[[Inferno Koi Shard]]|0.5|
|[[Dwarven Mines]]|GOOD CATCH|[[Abyssal Lanternfish Shard]]|0.5|
|Star Bait I - X ([[Attributes]])|GOOD CATCH|[[Shinyfish Shard]]|0.1-1|
|Lost and Found I - X ([[Attributes]])|GREAT CATCH|[[Wet Book]]|0.1-1|
|Fairy Pond ([[Wilderness]])|GOOD CATCH|[[Fairy Armor]] Piece|600|
Some items can increase amount of drops:
|Item|Effect|
|-|-|
|[[Cod Shard]] Cheapstake ([[Attributes]])|Gain +1-10% more [[Coins]] from fishing Treasures|
|[[Moray Eel Shard]] Lucky Rod ([[Attributes]])|Fish Treasure Shards ([[Attributes]]) are +1%-10% easier to find|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Frog Pet]]|Increases your chance to catch Water shards by 50% on Foraging Islands.|
|[[Large Fish Bowl]]|Doubles the odds of fishing a rare Attribute Shard ([[Attributes]]).|

|2nd, 9th and 24th treasure catch|Treasure|Fairy Soul ([[Fairy Souls]])||

|Item|Effect|
|-|-|

|[[Moray Eel Shard]] Lucky Rod ([[Attributes]])|Fish Treasure Shards ([[Attributes]]) are +1%-10% easier to find|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) [[Frog Pet]]|Increases your chance to catch Water shards by 50% on Foraging Islands.|
|[[Large Fish Bowl]]|Doubles the odds of fishing a rare Attribute Shard ([[Attributes]]).|

# UI Items/Barrier
Categories: More info needed, Glitches
Barrier
This is a list of [[Barrier]] UI items known to be in possession of players. Barriers in UI have a dark red name.
## Barriers
- Anvil
- Close
- Runic Pedestal
- Drill Anvil
- Recipe Required
- Error!
## Obtaining
### Anvil
Anvil Barrier can be obtained by switching to Touchscreen Mode in settings, opening [[Anvil]], then dragging the "Combine Items" in your inventory, and dragging the output into your inventory again. This will result in Anvil Barrier appearing in your inventory.
### Close
Close Barrier can be obtained from the [[Fishing Bag]] in a co-op. By opening the bag and having your co-op member unlock the next tier of the Fishing Bag while still having the Fishing Bag opened, it becomes possible to obtain the Close Barrier.
### Runic Pedestal
[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
### Drill Anvil
The Drill Anvil barrier can be obtained by fueling a drill with Touch Screen mode on. This method is the same as the anvil except at the drill anvil instead.
### Recipe Required
It is believed that only one exists, and was previously obtained from the [[Crafting Table]] UI the moment update that introduced Quick Crafting came out. Currently in possession of TimeDeo.
### Error!
It is believed that only one exists, obtained from Enchantment Table. Currently in possession of Anomaly Archive museum.
## Descriptions
### Anvil
"Place a target item in the left
slot and a sacrifice item in the
right slot to combine
Enchantments!"
### Drill Anvil
"Place a Drill in the left slot
and Drill Fuel or a Drill
Part in the right slot!"
### Runic Pedestal
"Place a target item in the left
slot and a sacrifice rune in the
right slot to add the rune's
effects to the item or add two
runes to attempt to fuse them!"
### Recipe Required
"Add the items for a valid
recipe in the crafting grid
to the left!"
### Error!
"You cannot add that enchantment
to that item!"
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Unknown Date|Players started obtaining Barrier UI Items.|
Linked from: [[UI Items]]

# Undead (Mob Type)
Categories: More info needed, Mob Types
[Undead (Mob Type)]
Type: Mob Type ([[Mob Types]])
Enemies of this type are typically found in the Skyblock Hub.
— Ike
Undead is a [[Mob Types|Mob Type]]. The [[Smite]] enchantment and the [[Attributes|Undead Ruler]] attribute both specifically increase damage against Undead mobs.

## Increasing stats against Undead
There are many ways to increase stats against Undead enemies.

|[[Undead Sword]]|[[Undead Sword]]|Sword ([[Swords]])|Deal 100% [[Damage]] to Undead mobs.|Multiplicative (Multiplicative buffs are simple multipliers applied at the end, after flat and additive buffs.)|

# Vanilla Objects/Blocks
Aliases: Any Fuel, Any fuel, Creeper Skull, Fire, Iron Bars, Lava, Vanilla Blocks, Water, Wither Skeleton Skull, Zombie Skull
Categories: More info needed
Vanilla Objects are items/mobs/blocks in vanilla (unmodified) Minecraft, and are in the game, but have little to no special purpose specific to SkyBlock, and as such are listed here to avoid page clutter. If vanilla items have an important enough SkyBlock-specific purpose a page should be made for them instead. Please visit the link to the Minecraft wiki's page on it for more details.

|[[Block of Diamond]]|[[Block of Diamond]]||▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Diamond]] ×9|Can be crafted into [[Enchanted Diamond]] (160x Blocks of Diamond → 9x Enchanted Diamond) (requires Diamond IV (4) ([[Diamond]])). See also: [[Enchanted Diamond Block]]|
|[[Block of Emerald]]|[[Block of Emerald]]||▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Emerald]] ×9|Can be crafted into [[Enchanted Emerald]] (160x Blocks of Emerald → 9x Enchanted Emerald) (requires Emerald IV (4) ([[Emerald]])). See also: [[Enchanted Emerald Block]]|
|[[Block of Lapis Lazuli]]|[[Block of Lapis Lazuli]]||▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Lapis Lazuli]] ×9|Can be crafted into [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli]] (160x Block of Lapis Lazuli → 9x Enchanted Lapis Lazuli ) (requires Lapis Lazuli IV (4) ([[Lapis Lazuli]])). 1 is used in crafting the [[Weather Node]].; See also: [[Enchanted Lapis Lazuli Block]]|
|[[Block of Redstone]]|[[Block of Redstone]]||▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Redstone Dust]] ×9|Can be crafted into Enchanted Redstone ([[Enchanted Redstone Dust]]) (160x Block of Redstone → 9x Enchanted Redstone ) (requires Redstone IV (4) ([[Redstone Dust]])).; See also: [[Enchanted Redstone Block]]|
|[[Furnace]]|[[Furnace]]||▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Cobblestone]] ×8|It was once possible to use SkyBlock items as their vanilla counterpart.; For example, a [[Necron's Handle]] was able to be used as a fuel source because it is considered a [[Stick]]. 1 is used in creating the Fireplace ([[Furniture]]) and one in the Furnace+ ([[Furniture]]).; See also: [[Auto Smelter]] (for Minions), [[Hyper Furnace]]|
|[[Noteblock]]|[[Noteblock]]||▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Planks]] ×8 + [[Redstone Dust]]|Can be found in the [[Bazaar Alley]]. 1 is used in crafting the [[Silence Block]].|
|[[Jukebox]]|[[Jukebox]]||▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Planks]] ×8 + [[Diamond]]|Can also play custom SkyBlock Discs, such as the [[Spooky Disc]], [[Battle Disc]], and [[Winter Disc]].; No matter how far you are from the jukebox that is playing the disc, you will hear it at the same volume across your island.; Also used to craft [[Crop Fever]] books and the [[Buzzybee's Fantabulous Disco Destination]].|
|[[Cauldron]]|[[Cauldron]]||▦ Recipe Recipe: [[Iron Ingot]] ×7||
|[[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]|Fire|||[[Crimson Isle]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Dwarven Mines]] [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.); Deals [More Info Needed] damage, wearing [[Ember Armor]] or having a [[Fire Talisman]] gives immunity (on [[Crimson Isle]], reduces damage taken by 20; doesn't work in [[Crystal Hollows]]).|
|[[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]|Lava|||[[Deep Caverns]] (entrance and the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]]), [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Blazing Fortress]], [[Crimson Isle]], and Winter Island ([[Jerry's Workshop]]).; Deals 25 damage, wearing [[Ember Armor]] or having a [[Lava Talisman]] gives immunity (on [[Crimson Isle]], reduces damage taken by 20; doesn't work in [[Crystal Hollows]]). See also: [[Lava Bucket]], [[Magical Lava Bucket]]|
|[[Vanilla Objects/Blocks]]|Water|||Found on most islands in SkyBlock See also: [[Water Bucket]], [[Magical Water Bucket]]|

The Albino Scatha Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Albino Scatha Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 10, 2023. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 8,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Albino Scatha Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Scatha Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance to an animated version.
- This skin is animated!

The Baby Spinosaurus Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Baby Spinosaurus Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on May 16, 2026. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 5,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Baby Spinosaurus Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Spinosaurus Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 21 different animated versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 21 cute baby Spinos!

Beach Dye is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] Animated [[Dyes|Dye]].
## Obtaining
The Beach Dye can be purchased from the Summer 2026 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it costs 375 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Frog pets.; This skin will allow you to swap between 15 biome themed frogs!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; MYTHIC COSMETIC
The Biome Frog Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that can be obtained from a [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Biome Frog Skin can be purchased from the Fall 2025 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it costs 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]].
## Usage
The Biome Frog Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Frog Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 15 different animated versions.
- This skin will allow you to swap between 15 biome themed frogs!

Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Item skins give your gear a fresh new look! Apply them just like an enchant book using the anvil.; This skin can be applied to Umberella.; This skin is animated, and adapts to the day-night cycle!; MYTHIC COSMETIC
The Blood Moon Fishing Deployable Skin is an [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Fishing]] [[Deployable Skins|Deployable Skin]] that could be purchased from the Hypixel Store.
## Obtaining
The Blood Moon Fishing Deployable Skin could be purchased from the Hypixel Store during a 2026 Hypixel Store Halloween promotion.
## Usage
The Blood Moon Fishing Deployable Skin can be applied to a [[Umberella]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance to an animated version.
- This skin is animated, and adapts to day-night cycle!
## Gallery
- Day
- Night
## Trivia
- The name of this item is likely a reference to Terraria's mechanic, commonly called Blood Moon Fishing during which players uniquely can fish up enemies.
  - Terraria's Blood Moon is based on a common colloquial name for the Moon during a total eclipse.

The Celestial Necron's Helmet Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Celestial Necron's Helmet Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on December 9, 2022. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 8,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Celestial Necron's Helmet Skin can be applied to a [[Necron's Armor|Necron's Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance to an animated version.
- This skin is animated!

- Celestial Necron's Helmet Skin

- Despite being only applicable to [[Necron's Armor|Necron's Helmet]], once applied the helmet can be converted to any of the other [[Wither Armor|Wither Helmet]] upgrades, keeping the [[Celestial Necron's Helmet Skin]] Celestial Necron's Helmet Skin on them.

# Chocolate Syringe

[Chocolate Syringe]
Type: Item
Rarity: MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]

Auctionable: ✔ Yes

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PET_ITEM_CHOCOLATE_SYRINGE
Tooltip: Use at Kat to upgrade Rabbit Pets to Mythic rarity.; MYTHIC
The Chocolate Syringe is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] item used to upgrade the [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Rabbit Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
## Obtaining
The Chocolate Syringe can be purchased from the [[Chocolate Factory|Chocolate Shop]] for 10,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]] after reaching Chocolate Factory V.
## Usage
The Chocolate Syringe can be given to [[Kat]] as well as 100,000 [[Coins]] at [Lv1] to upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Rabbit Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]].
The [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Rabbit Pet]] unlocks the following perk:
|Perk|[Lv1]|[Lv100]|
|-|-|-|
|Chocolate Injections|Increases Chocolate Factory production by +0.01x. Duplicate Chocolate Rabbits that you find grant +1.3% Chocolate.|Increases Chocolate Factory production by +0.05x. Duplicate Chocolate Rabbits that you find grant +33% Chocolate|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Apr 9, 2024|Added Chocolate Syringe.|
|Sep 11, 2024|Added Chocolate Syringe to the [[Bazaar]].|
|Jan 20, 2026|0.24.1 RC|Increased the rarity of Chocolate Syringe from LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Apr 24, 2024|0.20.1|Added Chocolate Syringe.|
|Jan 27, 2026|0.24.1|Increased the rarity of Chocolate Syringe from LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]) to MYTHIC ([[Rarity]]).|
|Apr 28, 2026|0.24.4|Chocolate Syringe can no longer be recombobulated.|

The Cute Jellyfish Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on March 29, 2025. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 7,500 purchases.
## Usage
The Cute Jellyfish Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Jellyfish Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on the pet as an item will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 14 different animated versions.

The Dark Scatha Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Dark Scatha Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 10, 2023. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 8,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Dark Scatha Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Scatha Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance to an animated version.
- This skin is animated!

## Usage
Drill:
Divan's Drill [[Damage]]: +75 [[Breaking Power]]: +10 [[Mining Speed]]: +1,800 [[Mining Fortune]]: +150
The Divan's Drill has a base 3,000 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]], which can be increased through a [[Drills|Fuel Tank]]. Additionally, a [[Drills|Drill Engine]] and [[Drills|Upgrade Module]] can be added to the drill through a [[Drill Anvil]] to grant further bonuses.
When breaking blocks, the Divan's Drill's [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] will deplete. Most resources will remove 1 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] when mined; [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]], [[Gemstone|Onyx]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]] remove 2 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead; [[Glacite]], [[Umber]] and [[Tungsten]] remove 3 [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] instead. [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] can be regained by adding Fuel to the Drill through a [[Drill Anvil]].
## Tips
- The 5th slot of the drill is a universal mining slot, and can have either [[Gemstone|Topaz]], [[Gemstone|Jade]] or [[Gemstone|Amber]] applied to it.
- [[Divan's Powder Coating]] can be applied to this item for +500 [[Mining Speed]].
## Trivia
- It is the third item that has a base [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] rarity, after the [[Plasma Bucket]] and [[Cake Soul|Cake Souls]]
- This item requires the fourth highest amount of raw materials at 12,027,076.[Confirm (Confirmation needed; Is it accounting for 0.20.6?; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- The total time it takes to forge the drill and all of its components is 3438 hours (143 days 6 hours), or 2406.6 hours (100 days 6.6 hours) with [[Heart of the Mountain|Quick Forge]] 20.[Confirm]
- Prior 0.17.2, [[Divan's Drill]] Divan's Drill was needed to obtain the [[Kuudra Relic]]. The requirement has been changed to any [[Drills|Drill]].
- Divan's Drill is tied in fourth for having the most gemstone slots with [[Mastiff Armor]], [[Armor of Divan]] and [[Gemstone Gauntlet]], with all of the aforementioned falling behind the [[Artifact of Power]], [[Relic of Power]], and [[Shimmersparkle Chestplate]].
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Divan's Drill.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Divan's Drill.|
|Jul 27, 2021|Added +200 [[Mining Speed]] (from 1,600 to 1,800) and +30 [[Mining Fortune]] (from 120 to 150).|
|Jul 28, 2023|Added +5 [[Damage]] (from 70 to 75).|
|Apr 9, 2024|0.20|Reduced the Fuel ([[Machine Fuel]]) consumption of Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]) from 2-->1.|
|Apr 24, 2024|0.20.1|[[Glacite]], [[Umber]] and [[Tungsten]] now remove 3 Fuel ([[Machine Fuel]]) instead of 1.|
|Aug 6, 2024|0.20.5|Aquamarine ([[Gemstone]]), Citrine ([[Gemstone]]), Onyx ([[Gemstone]]) and Peridot ([[Gemstone]]) remove 2 Fuel ([[Machine Fuel]]) instead of 1.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Changed time it takes to forge Divan's Drill from 60 hours to 30 seconds.|
|Oct 15, 2024|0.20.7|Moved Divan's Drill from Special to Rarities in the [[Museum]].|

The Dumpster Diver Helmet Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that can be obtained from a [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]].
## Obtaining
The Dumpster Diver Helmet Skin can be purchased from the Winter 2025 [[Taylor|Seasonal Bundle]] from [[Taylor]]. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it costs 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]].[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
## Usage
The Dumpster Diver Helmet Skin can be applied to a [[Diver's Armor|Diver's Mask]] or [[Abyssal Armor|Abyssal Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance. Once applied, left-clicking on it will open a menu, allowing for the skin to be changed between 20 different animated versions.
- This skin is swappable between 20 dumpster divin' variants!

Tooltip: Consumed on use; Pet skins change the look and particle trail of your pet but only one skin can be active at a time!; This skin can only be applied to Armadillo pets.; This skin is animated!; Right-click on your summoned pet to apply this skin!; MYTHIC COSMETIC
The Enchanted Armadillo Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Pet Skins|Pet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Enchanted Armadillo Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on February 16, 2022. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 10,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Enchanted Armadillo Skin can be applied to the player's summoned [[Armadillo Pet]] by right-clicking it to change its particle trail and appearance to an animated version.
- This skin is animated!

[Item Metadata]
Item ID: ENCHANTED_TURBO_GOURD
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; When applied to Farming Tool, increases ALL Turbo-Crop VI enchantments to Turbo-Crop VII.; Requires at least one Turbo-Crop VI enchantment!; MYTHIC
An Enchanted Turbo Gourd is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Endcaps|Endcap Upgrade]] that can be applied to any [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] with [[Turbo-Crop|Turbo-Crop VI]] to upgrade it to [[Turbo-Crop|Turbo-Crop VII]].
## Obtaining
### Crafting
|Requirement|Ingredients|[[Crafting]] recipe|
|-|-|-|
|None|5x [[Turbo Gourd]]|Recipe: [[Turbo Gourd]] ×5 → Enchanted Turbo Gourd|
## Usage
When applying an Enchanted Turbo Gourd to any [[Farming Tools|Farming Tool]] in an Anvil, all Turbo-Crop enchantments present at the time of applying are upgraded from Turbo-Crop VI to Turbo-Crop VII.

The Ender Knight Skin is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] [[Helmet Skins|Helmet Skin]] that could be purchased from a [[Fire Sale]].
## Obtaining
The Ender Knight Skin could be purchased from the [[Fire Sale]] hosted on October 7, 2023. Being [[Rarity|MYTHIC]], it cost 1,350 [[SkyBlock Gems]] and had a limited stock of 8,000 purchases.
## Usage
The Ender Knight Skin can be applied to a [[Final Destination Armor|Final Destination Helmet]] by combining it in an [[Anvil]] to change its appearance to an animated version.

Item ID: ENDERMAN_CORTEX_REWRITER
Tooltip: Use at Kat to upgrade Enderman Pets to Mythic rarity.; MYTHIC
The Enderman Cortex Rewriter is a [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] item used to upgrade the [[Rarity]] of an [[Enderman Pet]] from [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] at [[Kat]].
## Obtaining
### Mob Drops
|Level|Mob|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv305]|[[Voidling Extremist]]|1|8Legendary Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|0.1%|
## Usage
Using this item can upgrade a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Enderman Pet]] to [[Rarity|MYTHIC]] at [[Kat]] for 1,000,000 [[Coins]]. The process will take 1 hour.
